function u56.start()
    -- Line: 1048
    -- upvalues: buildPanel (val), buildPublish (val), buildJoin (val), BuildController (val), u56 (val), u103 (ref)
    -- upvalues: u123 (ref), PanelTabs (val), u84 (ref), Categories (val), u96 (ref), render (val), u76 (val)
    -- upvalues: showPublish (val), u121 (ref), u120 (ref), Ui (val), u101 (ref), u105 (ref), u95 (ref), u90 (ref)
    -- upvalues: renderPublishing (val), renderCode (val), renderStats (val), u79 (val)
    buildPanel()
    buildPublish()
    buildJoin()
    BuildController.registerMenu("Showrooms", u56.close)
    BuildController.registerClose("ShowroomPublish", u103)
    BuildController.registerClose("ShowroomJoin", u123)

    PanelTabs.register(
        function() -- Line: 1061; upvalues: u84 (upval)
            return u84
        end,
        function(a1) -- Line: 1063; upvalues: Categories (upval), PanelTabs (upval), u96 (upval), render (upval)
            local v1 = {"All", (table.unpack(Categories))}
            local v2 = u96 or "All"
            local v3 = PanelTabs.neighbour(v1, v2, a1)

            if v3 then
                u96 = if v3 ~= "All" then v3 else nil
                render()
            end
        end
    )

    u76.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(a1)
        -- Line: 1072
        -- upvalues: showPublish (upval), u121 (upval), u123 (upval), u56 (upval), u120 (upval), Ui (upval)
        -- upvalues: u101 (upval), u103 (upval), u105 (upval), u95 (upval), render (upval), u90 (upval)
        -- upvalues: renderPublishing (upval), renderCode (upval), renderStats (upval), u84 (upval)
        if type(a1) ~= "table" then
            return
        end

        local publish = a1.publish

        if type(publish) == "table"
            and type(publish.title) == "string"
            and type(publish.category) == "string" then
            showPublish({
                title = publish.title,
                category = publish.category,
                update = publish.update == true,
            })
            return
        end

        local joinResult = a1.joinResult

        if type(joinResult) == "table" then
            u121 = false

            if joinResult.ok then
                u123()
                u56.close()

                return
            end

            u120.Text = if type(joinResult.message) ~= "string" then
                "That didn't work"
            else
                joinResult.message
            u120.TextColor3 = Ui.Tone.Bad

            return
        end

        local publishResult = a1.publishResult

        if type(publishResult) == "table" then
            u101 = false

            if publishResult.ok then
                u103()
                return
            end

            u105.Text = if type(publishResult.message) ~= "string" then
                "That didn't work"
            else
                publishResult.message
            u105.TextColor3 = Ui.Tone.Bad

            return
        end

        if type(a1.cards) ~= "table" then
            return
        end

        u95 = a1.cards
        render()

        u90.Text = if type(a1.notice) ~= "string" then "" else a1.notice
        renderPublishing(a1.publishing)
        renderCode(a1.code)
        renderStats(a1.stats)

        if a1.open and not u84 then
            u56.show()
        end
    end)

    task.spawn(function() -- Line: 1121; upvalues: u84 (upval), u79 (upval)
        while true do
            task.wait(15)

            if u84 then
                u79:FireServer("poll")
            end
        end
    end)
end

return u56

local function swimmableSurface(a1)
    -- Line: 68
    -- upvalues: waterAt (val), CellSize (val), Config (val)
    -- types: a1: vector
    local v1 = waterAt(a1)

    if not v1 then
        return nil
    end

    local v2 = v1.Position - Vector3.new(0, v1.Size.Y / 2, 0)
        + Vector3.new(0, CellSize / 2, 0)
    local v3 = 1
    local v4 = v2

    while v3 < Config.Player.Swim.MinDepthCells do
        v4 = v4 - Vector3.new(0, CellSize, 0)

        if not waterAt(v4) then
            break
        end

        v3 = v3 + 1
    end

    v4 = v1
    v1 = v2

    while true do
        v1 = v1 + Vector3.new(0, CellSize, 0)
        v2 = waterAt(v1)

        if not v2 then
            break
        end

        v4 = v2
        v3 = v3 + 1
    end

    if v3 < Config.Player.Swim.MinDepthCells then
        return nil
    end

    return v4.Position.Y + v4.Size.Y / 2
end

    -- upvalues: u48 (ref), u33 (val), u49 (ref), Config (val)

local function step()
    -- Line: 169
    -- upvalues: LocalPlayer (val), stopSwimming (val), u42 (val), swimmableSurface (val), u48 (ref), u49 (ref)
    -- upvalues: startSwimming (val), u50 (ref), Config (val)
    local Character = LocalPlayer.Character
    local Humanoid = Character and Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    local RootPart = Humanoid and Humanoid.RootPart

    if Character and Humanoid and RootPart and not (Humanoid.Health <= 0) then
        if not Humanoid.PlatformStand and not Humanoid.Sit then
            local LookVector
            u42.FilterDescendantsInstances = {Character}
            local v1 = swimmableSurface(RootPart.Position)
            local v2 = u48

            if not v1 then
                stopSwimming(false)
                return
            end

            if not v2 then
                if os.clock() < u49 then
                    return
                end

                startSwimming(Humanoid, RootPart)

                v2 = u48
            end

            if not v2 then
                return
            end

            local Jump = Humanoid.Jump or os.clock() < u50

            if not Jump and (Humanoid:GetState()) ~= Enum.HumanoidStateType.Swimming then
                Humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Swimming)
            end

            local Swim = Config.Player.Swim
            local v3 = v1 - Swim.SurfaceDepthStuds

            if Jump then
                local Y = RootPart.Position.Y

                if v3 - 0.5 <= Y then
                    stopSwimming(true)
                    return
                end
            end

            v1 = Humanoid.WalkSpeed * Swim.SpeedFactor
            local MoveDirection = Humanoid.MoveDirection
            local CurrentCamera = workspace.CurrentCamera
            local v4 = Vector3.new((if not CurrentCamera then Vector3.new(0, 0, 1) else CurrentCamera.CFrame.LookVector).X, 0, LookVector.Z)
            local v5 = if not (0.01 < v4.Magnitude) then 0 else MoveDirection:Dot(v4.Unit)
            local RiseSpeed = LookVector.Y * v1 * math.max(v5, 0)

            if Jump then
                RiseSpeed = Swim.RiseSpeed
            elseif MoveDirection.Magnitude < 0.1 then
                RiseSpeed = Swim.FloatSpeed
            end

            if v3 <= RootPart.Position.Y and RiseSpeed > 0 then
                RiseSpeed = math.min(RiseSpeed, (v3 - RootPart.Position.Y) * 4)
            end

            v2.velocity.VectorVelocity = MoveDirection * v1 + Vector3.new(0, RiseSpeed, 0)

            return
        end

        stopSwimming(false)

        return
    end

    stopSwimming(false)
end

        u50 = os.clock() + 0.2
    end)

    RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(step)

    LocalPlayer.CharacterRemoving:Connect(function() -- Line: 240; upvalues: u48 (upval)
        u48 = nil
    end)
end

return v1

local function requiredToolFor(a1, a2)
    -- Line: 214
    -- upvalues: CollectionService (val), Config (val)
    -- types: a1: userdata, a2: string?
    if a1:GetAttribute("Indestructible") then
        return nil, false
    end

    if a1:GetAttribute("BushCell") then
        return if a2 ~= "Axe" then "Pickaxe" else "Axe", false
    end

    local v1 = a1:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")
    local PrimaryPart = if not v1 then nil else v1.PrimaryPart

    if a1:GetAttribute("FlowerCell") then
        return "Pickaxe", false
    end

    if PrimaryPart
        and PrimaryPart:GetAttribute("FlowerCell")
        and CollectionService:HasTag(PrimaryPart, "Flower") then
        return "Pickaxe", false
    end

    if a1:GetAttribute("CropCell") then
        local PrimaryPart_2 = a1
        v1 = a1:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")

        if v1 and v1.PrimaryPart then
            PrimaryPart_2 = v1.PrimaryPart
        end

        if CollectionService:HasTag(PrimaryPart_2, "RipeCrop") and a2 ~= nil then
            return a2, false
        end

        return "Pickaxe", false
    end

    local Attribute = a1:GetAttribute("NodeType")

    if Attribute then
        local v2 = Config.Tool.Nodes[Attribute]
        return if not v2 then nil else v2.requires, Config.Player.AutoEquipForTarget
    end

    if a1:GetAttribute("CartId") or a1:GetAttribute("CellKey") then
        return "Pickaxe", false
    end

    return nil, false
end

local function actionIcon(a1) -- Line: 116; upvalues: Config (val)

    if a1.kind == "Tool" then
        return ActionIcons[a1.toolClass or ""] or ActionIcons.Use
    end

    local itemId = a1.itemId

    if a1.kind == "Item" and itemId then
        if Config.Food.isFood(itemId) then

        end

            return ActionIcons.Plow
        end

        if a1.placeable then
            return ActionIcons.Place
        end
    end

    return ActionIcons.Use
end

function sameApartFrom(a1, a2, a3) -- Line: 1378; upvalues: sameApartFrom (val), u202 (val); types: a3: table

            end

    end

                -- upvalues: picker (val), i12 (val), u248 (val), u154 (upval), u60 (upval), u144 (upval), u146 (upval)
                -- upvalues: u147 (upval), RunService (upval), u122 (upval), u142 (upval), pointerOn (upval)
                -- upvalues: u145 (upval)
                if not picker.shownOptions[i12].lit and u154[u248] ~= "SlotHover" then
                    u154[u248] = "SlotHover"

                local copy = picker.shownOptions[i12].copy

                u144(itemId, u248, copy)

                if u147 then
                    return
                end

                u147 = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
                    -- Line: 171
                    -- upvalues: u146 (upval), u122 (upval), u142 (upval), pointerOn (upval), u147 (upval), u145 (upval)
                    local v1 = u146

                    if not v1
                        or not u122
                        or not u142
                        or not v1:IsDescendantOf(u142)
                        or not pointerOn(v1) then
                        u146 = nil

                            u147:Disconnect()

                            u147 = nil
                        end

                    end

            end)

            v2.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
                -- Line: 1056
                -- upvalues: u248 (val), picker (val), i12 (val), u154 (upval), u60 (upval), u146 (upval), u147 (upval)
                -- upvalues: u145 (upval)
                local v1 = u248
                local v2 = if not picker.shownOptions[i12].lit then "Slot" else "SlotSelected"

                if u154[v1] ~= v2 then
                    u154[v1] = v2
                    u60.paint(v1, v2)
                end

                if u147 then
                    u147:Disconnect()

                    u147 = nil
                end

            end)
        end
    end

    if #v10 == 0 then
        picker.note.Text = "Nothing carried fits this slot."
        picker.note.Visible = true
    end
end
